Program
Information
JUNE 5–8, 2026 | NEW ORLEANS, LA


ADA2026.ORG | #ADASCISESSIONS
Welcome to the American
Diabetes Association’s
2026 Scientific Sessions
in New Orleans.
As the world’s largest meeting on diabetes,
the Scientific Sessions is both a leading
educational gathering and a unique
opportunity to network with colleagues
from over 115 countries. We couldn’t be
more excited to reconnect and accelerate
progress towards preventing and curing
diabetes and improving the lives of all
people affected by diabetes.

   Pushing the Limits of What Sensing Can Do for T1D and T2D

   Provided by CMEducation Resources.


   Supported by an educational grant from Abbott Diabetes Care.


   Using international authorities on diabetes management as faculty, this activity will focus on emerging digital,

   drug interventions for diabetes, minimize risks of hypoglycemia and DKA, maximize time in target range for

                                           6:30 p.m. - 9:30 p.m.
   Obesity: New and Emerging Strategies to Optimally Manage a Chronic Disease

   Provided by Physicians Education Resource (PER)

   This continuing education (CE) activity will help endocrinologists, diabetes specialists, and other healthcare
   providers who manage patients with obesity to stay current on the unmet needs in obesity care and how those
   needs are being addressed. This will include a review of the current guidelines for obesity management,
   methods for obesity assessment, recommendations for the use of currently approved anti-obesity medications
   (AOMs), and the current status of emerging agents to potentially expand the therapeutic armamentarium and
   increase the ability of clinicians to personalize therapy.

   On Track With Chronic Kidney Disease: The Race to Early Detection and Risk Reduction in Type 1 and
   Type 2 Diabetes

   Provided by Medscape Live Meeting Services, LLC
   Supported by an educational grant from Bayer US


   This fun and dynamic Amazing Case symposium will feature a quiz show where the live and virtual audience is
   separated into teams participating in a virtual race. Video clips from landmarks across the world, country, or
   city will play throughout the presentation, and teams will compete for first place by answering a series of case-
   based questions. The goal of this activity is to improve clinician competence and confidence in the early
   identification, risk stratification, and comprehensive management of CKD in patients with T1D or T2D,
   emphasizing the importance of guideline-recommended screening, interpretation of uACR and eGFR data, and
   integration of evidence-based, cardiorenal-protective treatment strategies to slow CKD progression and reduce
   CV events.

   Sarcopenia and Diabetes Care: Muscle Health and Targeted Nutrition

   Provided by an educational grant from Abbott Nutrition


   This symposium will explore the central role of skeletal muscle in metabolic health and diabetes outcomes,
   highlight evidence for diabetes specific nutrition formulas in improving glycemic control and supporting
   remission, and provide practical strategies for integrating targeted nutrition and muscle focused care into
   routine practice.
